Yesterday, I was walking in the park when I saw an old friend. He was sitting on a bench and reading a book. While we were talking, it started to rain, so we ran to a small café nearby. We drank coffee and laughed because we had not seen each other for a long time. While we were sitting there, more people were coming in because of the rain. A man was playing soft music, and everyone was listening quietly. My friend was telling me about his new job while I was enjoying the warm coffee. After the rain stopped, we left the café and went home. On my way home, I was thinking about our meeting and smiling. It was a simple day, but it made me very happy.

Exercise 1:
Create 5 sentences with true/false conditions

       Was Ben happy in the end of the walk? Yes
       Was Ben drinking coffee while he was telling Sam about new job ?
       Do they know each other ?
      Did they stay in the cafe after the rain stopped ?
      Was it a difficult day for Ben?
       

Exercise 2:

Yesterday, I walking in the park when I saw an old friend. He siting on a bench and reding a book. While were talking, it started to rain, so we run to a small café nearby. We drank coffee and laughed because were not seen each other for a long time. While we siting there, more people coming in because of the rain. A man playing soft music, and everyone listening quietly. My friend telling me about his new job while I enjoying the warm coffee. After the rain stopped , we left the café and went home. On my way home, I thinking about our meeting and smiled. It was a simple day, but it made me very happy.

Yesterday, I walk in the park when I saw an old friend. He sat on a bench and read 
 book. While we talking, it started to rain, so we ran to a small café nearby.
 We drinking coffee and laughing because we not saw each other for a long time.
 While we siting there, more people coming in because of the rain.
 A man playing soft music, and everyone listening quietly. My friend telling me about his new job while I enjoying the warm coffee.
 After the rain stopped , we left the café and going home. On my way home,
 I thinking about our meeting and smiled. It was a simple day, but it made me very happy. ^U5ZnvlzX
